# V8-0 inventory

Every path of the repository at `v7-final-art` (`b1ca032f`), with a verdict:
KEEP, DELETE, MOVE→path, ASK (kept; the owner decides), MIXED (a directory
whose files have different verdicts; the file rows follow) or the test
triage verdicts. Nothing was deleted without a row here (goal 4.4). The
verdict table at the end is generated by `tools/v8/inventory.py` from ordered
rules; `tools/v8/inventory.py check docs/v8/V8_0_INVENTORY.md` is the gate's
closure step (every `git ls-files` path is covered by a row, by prefix).

## 1. Method

### A. Rust live graph

- **Engine entry.** The game builds its engine in
  `rust/crates/sim-bridge/src/ffi/world_node.rs` `build_game_engine(seed)` =
  `build_world_with(&WorldGenConfig::game_v26(seed), WorldRules::game_v26(seed))`;
  `build_world_with` (`rust/crates/sim-systems/src/worldgen/mod.rs`) calls
  `register_default_runtime_systems` (`rust/crates/sim-systems/src/lib.rs`),
  which registers **every** system below. The bridge's 37 `#[func]` methods
  are listed in `docs/v8/bridge_api_v7_final.txt` (from `tools/v8/bridge_api.py`).
- **Systems.** Names, priorities and intervals are read from the engine at run
  time (`worldsim_perf` writes them per run: `$DATA/base/perf/natural_r1/*.json`
  `systems`), files by grepping each `fn name(&self)` literal. Share = the
  system's share of tick time in the base natural measurement.
- **Modules.** A symbol scan (`$DATA/rust_module_refs.txt`) found a user outside
  its own file for every `pub` item of every `sim-core` / `sim-systems` /
  `sim-engine` module, or the module is wired by its parent `mod` (private
  submodules such as `worldgen/camp.rs`, `routine/places.rs`). No module of
  those crates is unreachable from the bridge, so the compile check ("removing
  it breaks `cargo build -p sim-bridge`") holds for each by construction.
- **Behavioural check (ASK candidates).** Goal 6.3 names `influence/**`,
  `tile/**`, `runtime/influence/**`, `runtime/combat/**`, `runtime/memory/**`
  and `fission_threshold.rs`. All are registered or used by a registered
  system. To tell the owner whether each changes outcomes under `game_v26`,
  the base build was patched (in the base worktree only, reverted afterwards)
  to skip named systems, and the probe ran seeds 0–3 for 120 days; results in
  section 2.3.

### B. GDScript and scenes — `tools/v8/gd_reach.py`

Starts from `project.godot` (main scene, autoloads, theme, icon) and the
`.gdextension`, and follows `preload` / `load` / `extends "res://…"`, every
`res://` literal, `class_name` uses, `[ext_resource]` paths in `.tscn`/`.tres`,
`#include` in shaders and `res://` literals inside reached `.json` files (atlas
pages, terrain sheets). Formatted or concatenated literals become globs
(`res://localization/%s/` → `localization/*/*`). One derived-name rule:
`X.png` reaches `X_regions.png` (`art_sprite_library.gd` `_regions_for_page`).
Unit tests: `tools/v8/test_gd_reach.py` (5 hand-made projects).

- Whole game at base: 130 reachable files.
- Closure of the 7.3 keep set (seeds: `locale.gd`, the six kept renderers /
  controllers, the four kept panels, `icon.svg`, the theme, the extension):
  108 files — 37 scripts, 23 assets, 2 shaders, 43 localization files.

### C. Assets and shaders

Referenced by a reachable script, scene, resource or reached JSON manifest
(gd_reach above); Rust loads no `res://` asset (`grep -rn 'res://' rust/crates/*/src`
finds none). Kept: Galmuri fonts, the theme, the atlas pages + region maps,
terrain sheets + manifest, `contact_shadow.png`, `villager_ramps.png`, one
production PNG preloaded by a kept dependency, `villager_ramp.gdshader`,
`settlement_outline.gdshader`.

### D. Localization — `tools/v8/locale_keys.py`

A key is used when a string literal equal to it appears in a reachable `.gd`
file (comments stripped), when it matches a runtime-built family (`"X_%s"`
literals, `"PREFIX_" + x`, `x + "_SUFFIX"`), or when a Rust literal in the sim
or bridge crates equals it (keys the bridge would send).

- Universe: 5,287 keys (`localization/compiled/ko.json`).
- Whole game at base: 197 used (189 literals + the `GOD_HAND_*` family).
- 7.3 keep set: **157 keys**, all literals; no runtime-built family, no
  Rust-sent key.
- The runtime table is generated from Fluent sources today (see DECISIONS D14);
  V8 keeps the category JSONs as the source.

### 2.2 sim-core modules reached

`causal/**` (event log, ring buffer, storage — the "why?" panel), `chronicle.rs`,
`components/**` (30 component modules, all used by systems or the bridge),
`fission_threshold.rs` (settlement + fission systems), `influence/**` and
`tile/**` (influence grid and tile grid: worldgen, influence systems, movement),
`material/**` (registry built by `SimEngine::new`; data model kept for M2 —
not behaviourally live), `rng.rs`, `rules.rs` + `rules/routine.rs`,
`variety.rs` (`WorldVariety::draw_v18` feeds `game_v26` rules and worldgen).

### 2.3 ASK candidates — registered, and do they change outcomes?

<!-- ablation results inserted below -->

Method: in the base worktree only, `SimEngine::register_system` was patched
to skip systems named in `WORLDSIM_SKIP_SYSTEMS` (reverted afterwards, `git
status` clean); probe `--scene game_v26 --seeds 0..4 --days 120`; per-seed
summary compared with the unablated control (timing keys excluded; the
control equals the base identity data on seeds 0–3 except the clock-sample
blocks, which depend on the `--clock-samples` flag the control did not pass).
Raw results: `$DATA/ablation/`.

| candidate | registered in the live engine? | skipped systems | outcome change (seeds 0–3, 120 days) | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sim-core/src/influence/**`, `sim-systems/src/runtime/influence/**` | yes (priorities 90, 100, 110, 1000) | BuildingStamp + InfluenceUpdate + AgentInfluenceSample + InfluenceVisualization | **yes** — every seed; max alive 78/32/46/27 → 23/23/21/26 | KEEP (live: needs/decisions read the influence grid) |
| `runtime/influence/visualization.rs` alone | yes (1000, every 6 ticks) | InfluenceVisualization | none (display-only) | KEEP (feeds `get_influence_overlay`; bridge API) |
| `sim-core/src/tile/**` | used by worldgen, movement, influence, the bridge's terrain snapshot | — (data, cannot be skipped) | — | KEEP |
| `sim-systems/src/runtime/combat/**` | yes (137) | Combat | **none** in these runs | **ASK** — inert under `game_v26` in 4×120 days; M5 (animal threats, drafting) may reuse it |
| `sim-systems/src/runtime/memory/**` | yes (136) | Memory | **yes** — every seed (seed 2 max alive 46 → 43) | KEEP |
| `runtime/social/**` (for comparison) | yes (134) | SocialInteraction | **yes** — max alive 78/32/46/27 → 26/25/23/27 | KEEP |
| `sim-core/src/fission_threshold.rs` | used by SettlementSystem and FissionSystem (148) | — | fission happens in base worlds (1–3 settlements per world) | KEEP |

## 3. Test triage and coverage

<!-- triage summary inserted below -->

Triage of the 151 files in `rust/crates/sim-test/tests/` by a fresh read-only reviewer (Part 1), following goal 6.5. Facts it rests on (default rules switch off the 8 rules-gated systems; only one file used `game_v26`; 19 files import the deleted `worldmodel::digest::state_digest`) and the adaptation recipes R1–R3 are summarised here; the 8.3 ruling is DECISIONS D15. The rows are the source of the test verdicts in the generated table (section 5).

### 3.0 Rules and recipes


- **DELETE** a file in any of these cases:
  - Its subject is UI or locale source text: static string assertions on `.gd`,
    `.tscn`, `localization/*`, or the pipeline-delegated stubs.
  - It targets a deleted feature: interventions, worldmodel, `.harness`, deleted UI.
  - It is a frozen pin, a `vN_unchanged` comparison, or `inert_before_vN`.
  - It only exercises behaviour that exists solely in a non-`game_v26` scene or
    version.
  - It duplicates a kept test.
- **Rust-source text greps.** I treat static assertions on `.rs` source text (for
  example "`world_node.rs` contains `self.engine.resources.current_tick`") like
  UI-text assertions: they are scaffolding that the 8.2 renames break, and compile
  and behaviour tests cover the same ground. **This extends the brief and is my
  judgment;** each such fn is named in its row.
- **Old-version scene runs.** A scene test whose subject is a version delta, a pin or
  a seed-tuned outcome is **deleted**. A scene test whose subject is a
  version-independent invariant of a live feature is **KEEP-ADAPT (R3)**, ported to
  the baseline scene. Examples of such invariants: "villagers sleep in beds at night",
  "children mature on schedule", "chronicle rebuilds rosters", "noise fork with
  replicate 0 is the identity". This reads the brief's KEEP-ADAPT case (a kept test
  calling a deleted constructor) as covering scene constructors.

### Adaptation recipes

- **R1 (rebase on the baseline).** In a `SimEngine::new` unit fixture, replace
  `WorldRules::game_vN()` and `game_vN(seed).x` with `WorldRules::baseline(seed)`, or
  with the needed sub-rules copied from it into `WorldRules::default()`. Derive
  expected numbers from the rule (`rule.burn_per_hour[…]`), not from literals.
  - **Exact** (same values): when the copied fields did not change between vN and
    v26. That holds for `night_rest`, `evening_gather`, `life_stage`,
    `shelter_planning`, `meals`, `forage` (v11+), `stock_target` (v11+), `temperament`,
    `social_seek`, `need_fallthrough`, `critical_preemption`, the v20 `firewood`, and
    the fission group fields.
  - **Re-verify** in every other case.
- **R2 (test-local literal of an old rule value).** Use only for numeric parameters.
  **Never use it for an `Option` or `bool` flag whose non-baseline side GOAL 8.3 might
  delete**, because a kept test setting the other value blocks that removal (8.3,
  condition 2). Rows that would need R2 on a flag say so and delete the fn instead.
- **R3 (port the scene to `baseline_scene(seed)`).** Drop the comparison arm against
  the old version. Re-measure any seed-specific count or threshold (founder count,
  deadwood count and capacity now come from `WorldVariety::draw_v18`). Replace
  `state_digest` with `probe::fingerprint`.

### 3.2 Coverage: live system → kept tests

> File names below are the V7 names. V8-0 8.4 renamed and merged them by domain; the old → new map is DECISIONS D26 (for example `harness_revitalize_pass20_relations` → `relations_rule`). Every system below still has its tests after the rename.


"Run" means the system ticks with its rule present, either directly
(`System::new().tick(…)`) or through a registry plus `engine.tick()`, and the test
asserts on its output. Under default rules, the 8 rules-gated systems tick but return
at once, so V7 default-rule files do not count for them.

Every file with `+reg` also ticks all 28 systems. `harness_revitalize_pass21_relations`
(`harness_p21_game_v26_invariants_and_twin`) ticks all 28 under live rules for 60 days,
and asserts only relations, fission ≥ 1 and tally-vs-chronicle.

Suffixes: (T) = KEEP-TRIM, (A) = KEEP-ADAPT, R3 = coverage exists only after the scene
port.
